        stretchClk.AutoButtonColor = false
        stretchClk.ZIndex = 10
        stretchClk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setStretch(not stretchOn) end)
        _G.stretchToggleSetter = setStretch
    end
    setAntiLagVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "Anti Lag", function(on)
        if on then enableAntiLag() else disableAntiLag() end
        antiLagEnabled = on == true
        pcall(saveAllSettings, true)
    end)
    do
        local row = mkRow(visualPage, 42)
        mkLabel(row, "Sky Theme")
        local container = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 1, 0)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 0, 0)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.ZIndex = 8
        local leftBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
        leftBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
        leftBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -13)
        leftBtn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        leftBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.10
        leftBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        leftBtn.Text = "<"
        leftBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        leftBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        leftBtn.TextSize = 13
        leftBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        leftBtn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", leftBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        skySelectorLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", container)
        skySelectorLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 26)
        skySelectorLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -48, 0.5, -13)
        skySelectorLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        skySelectorLabel.Text = skyTheme
        skySelectorLabel.TextColor3 = WHITE
        skySelectorLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        skySelectorLabel.TextSize = 11
        skySelectorLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        skySelectorLabel.ZIndex = 9
        local rightBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
        rightBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
        rightBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, -13)
        rightBtn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        rightBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.10
        rightBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rightBtn.Text = ">"
        rightBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        rightBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        rightBtn.TextSize = 13
        rightBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        rightBtn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", rightBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local function updateSkySelector(direction)
            local idx = 1
            for i, name in ipairs(SKY_PRESETS_LIST) do
                if name == skyTheme then idx = i; break end
            end
            local newIdx = idx + direction
            if newIdx < 1 then newIdx = #SKY_PRESETS_LIST end
            if newIdx > #SKY_PRESETS_LIST then newIdx = 1 end
            local name = SKY_PRESETS_LIST[newIdx]
            skyTheme = name
            pcall(applyCustomSky, name)
            if skySelectorLabel then skySelectorLabel.Text = name end
            pcall(saveAllSettings, true)
        end
        leftBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() updateSkySelector(-1) end)
        rightBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() updateSkySelector(1) end)
    end

    mkSect(visualPage, "Overlays")
    setESPVIsual = mkToggle(visualPage, "Player ESP", function(on) toggleESP(on) end)
    setESPLineVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "ESP Line", function(on)
        if on then startESPLine() else stopESPLine() end
    end)

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- KINGMSC  ·  CONFUSER + ANTI BAT + CUSTOM TOOLS  (portados del MCS_DUEL)
        -- Todo queda aislado en _G.KINGMSC y se engancha a las filas del panel.
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        local KINGMSC = _G.KINGMSC
        if type(KINGMSC) ~= "table" then
            KINGMSC = { confuser = {}, antiBat = {}, customTools = {} }
            _G.KINGMSC = KINGMSC
        end
        KINGMSC.confuser = type(KINGMSC.confuser) == "table" and KINGMSC.confuser or {}
        KINGMSC.antiBat = type(KINGMSC.antiBat) == "table" and KINGMSC.antiBat or {}
        KINGMSC.customTools = type(KINGMSC.customTools) == "table" and KINGMSC.customTools or {}
        KINGMSC.loaded = true
        KCF = KINGMSC.confuser
        KCT = KINGMSC.customTools
        KAB = KINGMSC.antiBat

        local KMS_RS  = game:GetService("RunService")
        local KMS_UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local KMS_ACCENT = getThemeColor()

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- 1) CONFUSER  (giro del personaje + anti-die, igual que en MCS_DUEL)
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        KCF.on = KCF.on == true
        KCF.steal = KCF.steal == true
        KCF.speed = math.max(0, tonumber(KCF.speed) or 50)
        KCF.spinConn = nil
        KCF.angle = 0
        KCF.dieConns = {}
        KCF.dieAdded = nil
        KCF.dieBeat = nil
        KCF.dieActive = false
        KCF.stealConn = nil
        KCF.setVisual = nil

        local function kmsChar()
            local ch = LP and LP.Character
            return ch, ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end

        local function kmsStartSpin()
            if KCF.spinConn then return end
            KCF.angle = 0
            KCF.spinConn = KMS_RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                if not KCF.on then return end
                local ch, root = kmsChar()
                if not root then return end
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = false end) end
                local step = KCF.speed * dt * 60
                KCF.angle = (KCF.angle + step) % 360
                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(KCF.angle), 0)
                pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
            end)
        end

        local function kmsStopSpin()
            if KCF.spinConn then
                pcall(function() KCF.spinConn:Disconnect() end)
                KCF.spinConn = nil
            end
            local ch, root = kmsChar()
            if ch then
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then pcall(function() hum.AutoRotate = true end) end
                if root then pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end) end
            end
        end

        local function kmsClearCharEntry(char, restore)
            local entry = KCF.dieConns[char]
            if entry then
                for _, c in ipairs(entry) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                KCF.dieConns[char] = nil
            end
            if restore and char and char.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum then
                        hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
                        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        local function kmsApplyAntiDie(char)
            if not char or not char.Parent then return end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            kmsClearCharEntry(char, false)
            hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
            local conns = {}
            table.insert(conns, hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
                if KCF.dieActive and KCF.dieConns[char] == conns and hum.Health <= 0 then
                    hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                end
            end))
            table.insert(conns, hum.Died:Connect(function()
                task.defer(function()
                    if KCF.dieActive and KCF.dieConns[char] == conns and hum and hum.Parent then
                        hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
                        hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                    end
                end)
            end))
            table.insert(conns, char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
                if not char.Parent then kmsClearCharEntry(char, false) end
            end))
            KCF.dieConns[char] = conns
        end

        local function kmsSetAntiDie(enabled)
            enabled = enabled == true
            if enabled == KCF.dieActive then return end
            KCF.dieActive = enabled
            if enabled then
                if not KCF.dieAdded then
                    KCF.dieAdded = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        if KCF.dieActive and char and char.Parent then kmsApplyAntiDie(char) end
                    end)
                end
                local ch = LP.Character
                if ch and ch.Parent and not KCF.dieConns[ch] then kmsApplyAntiDie(ch) end
                if not KCF.dieBeat then
                    KCF.dieBeat = KMS_RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if not KCF.dieActive then return end
                        local c = LP.Character
                        local hum = c and c.Parent and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if not hum then return end
                        if not KCF.dieConns[c] then kmsApplyAntiDie(c) end
                        if hum.BreakJointsOnDeath then hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end
                        if hum:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then
                            pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
                        end
                        if hum.Health <= 0 then hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth end
                    end)
                end
            else
                if KCF.dieBeat then
                    pcall(function() KCF.dieBeat:Disconnect() end)
                    KCF.dieBeat = nil
                end
                for ch in pairs(KCF.dieConns) do kmsClearCharEntry(ch, true) end
            end
        end

        local function kmsSetConfuser(on)
            KCF.on = on == true
            if KCF.on then
                kmsSetAntiDie(true)
                kmsStartSpin()
            else
                kmsStopSpin()
                kmsSetAntiDie(antiDieEnabled == true)
            end
            if KCF.setVisual then pcall(KCF.setVisual, KCF.on) end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end

        local function kmsCarrying()
            local ch = LP.Character
            return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
                or (ch ~= nil and ch:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
        end

        KCF.stealConn = task.spawn(function()
            while _G.falls4kNavidadRunning ~= false and KINGMSC.loaded do
                task.wait(0.15)
                if not KCF.steal then
                    if KCF.stealApplied then
                        KCF.stealApplied = false
                        if KCF.on then pcall(kmsSetConfuser, false) end
                    end
                else
                    local carrying = kmsCarrying()
                    if carrying and not KCF.stealApplied and not KCF.on then
                        KCF.stealApplied = true
                        pcall(kmsSetConfuser, true)
                    elseif not carrying and KCF.stealApplied then
                        KCF.stealApplied = false
                        pcall(kmsSetConfuser, false)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        KINGMSC.setConfuser = kmsSetConfuser
        KINGMSC.confuserActive = function() return KCF.on == true end

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- 2) ANTI BAT MSC  (batido de velocidad limpio + inf jump, igual a MCS)
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        KAB.on = KAB.on == true
        KAB.jump = KAB.jump == true
        KAB.range = math.clamp(tonumber(KAB.range) or 4000, 100, 50000)
        KAB.jumpBoost = 55
        KAB.jumpMinY = 35
        KAB.maxFall = -120
        KAB.conn = nil
        KAB.jumpConn = nil
        KAB.setVisual = nil
        KAB.setJumpVisual = nil
        KAB.rangeBox = nil

        local function kmsReadVel(part)
            local ok, v = pcall(function() return part.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
            if ok and typeof(v) == "Vector3" then return v end
            local ok2, v2 = pcall(function() return part.Velocity end)
            if ok2 and typeof(v2) == "Vector3" then return v2 end
            return nil
        end

        local function kmsWriteVel(part, vel)
            pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel end)
            pcall(function() part.Velocity = vel end)
        end

        local function kmsAbStop()
            if KAB.conn then
                pcall(function() KAB.conn:Disconnect() end)
                KAB.conn = nil
            end
        end

        local function kmsAbStart()
            if KAB.conn and KAB.conn.Connected then return end
            kmsAbStop()
            KAB.conn = KMS_RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not KAB.on then return end
                local ch = LP.Character
                if not ch then return end
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                local hrp = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                local v = kmsReadVel(hrp)
                if not v then return end
                local r = KAB.range
                kmsWriteVel(hrp, Vector3.new(math.random(-r, r), v.Y, math.random(-r, r)))
                KMS_RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
                if hrp and hrp.Parent and KAB.on then kmsWriteVel(hrp, v) end
            end)
        end

        local function kmsAbApplyJump(root)
            local v = kmsReadVel(root)
            if not v then return end
            if v.Y < KAB.jumpMinY then
                kmsWriteVel(root, Vector3.new(v.X, KAB.jumpBoost, v.Z))
            end
            if v.Y < KAB.maxFall then
                kmsWriteVel(root, Vector3.new(v.X, KAB.maxFall, v.Z))
            end
        end

        local function kmsAbStopJump()
            if KAB.jumpConn then
                pcall(function() KAB.jumpConn:Disconnect() end)
                KAB.jumpConn = nil
            end
        end

        local function kmsAbStartJump()
            if KAB.jumpConn and KAB.jumpConn.Connected then return end
            kmsAbStopJump()
            local okPre, pre = pcall(function() return KMS_RS.PreSimulation end)
            local sig = (okPre and pre) or KMS_RS.Heartbeat
            KAB.jumpConn = sig:Connect(function()
                if not KAB.jump then return end
                local ch = LP.Character
                if not ch then return end
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hum or not root then return end
                local held = KMS_UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space)
                    or KMS_UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
                    or (hum.Jump == true)
                if held then
                    kmsAbApplyJump(root)
                else
                    local v = kmsReadVel(root)
                    if v and v.Y < KAB.maxFall then
                        kmsWriteVel(root, Vector3.new(v.X, KAB.maxFall, v.Z))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function kmsAbSet(on)
            KAB.on = on == true
            if KAB.on then kmsAbStart() else kmsAbStop() end
            if KAB.setVisual then pcall(KAB.setVisual, KAB.on) end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end

        local function kmsAbSetJump(on)
            KAB.jump = on == true
            if KAB.jump then kmsAbStartJump() else kmsAbStopJump() end
            if KAB.setJumpVisual then pcall(KAB.setJumpVisual, KAB.jump) end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end

        local function kmsAbSetRange(v)
            KAB.range = math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(v) or 4000) + 0.5), 100, 50000)
            if KAB.rangeBox then KAB.rangeBox.Text = tostring(KAB.range) end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end

        KINGMSC.setAntiBat = kmsAbSet
        KINGMSC.setInfJump = kmsAbSetJump
        KINGMSC.setAntiBatRange = kmsAbSetRange
        KINGMSC.antiBatActive = function() return KAB.on == true end

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- 3) CUSTOM TOOLS  (skins locales del bate / medusa, igual a MCS)
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        do
            if KCT.api and KCT.api.shutdown then pcall(KCT.api.shutdown) end

            local ASSETS = {
                DiamondSword = {
                    cat = "Bat", label = "Diamond Sword",
                    mesh = "rbxassetid://8827558932", tex = "rbxassetid://8827558969",
                    scale = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
                    c0 = CFrame.new(-0.05, -0.1, -0.12) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), math.rad(180), 300),
                },
                Katana = {
                    cat = "Bat", label = "Katana",
                    mesh = "rbxassetid://13528902482", tex = "rbxassetid://13528902373",
                    scale = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4),
                    c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(270), math.rad(180), math.rad(180)),
                },
                Skull = {
                    cat = "Medusa", label = "Skull",
                    mesh = "rbxassetid://2050312704", tex = "rbxassetid://2050313393",
                    scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.65, -0.4) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), 0, 0),
                },
                GoldenDesertEagle = {
                    cat = "Medusa", label = "Gold Eagle",
                    mesh = "rbxassetid://430251413", tex = "rbxassetid://435840335",
                    scale = Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01),
                    c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.8) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), math.rad(180), 0),
                },
            }
            local SOUNDS = {
                Bat = { id = "rbxassetid://5713085119", skip = 0.2 },
                Medusa = { id = "rbxassetid://3102797479", doublePlay = true, delay = 0.5 },
            }
            local skins = {
                Bat = KCT.skinBat or "DiamondSword",
                Medusa = KCT.skinMedusa or "GoldenDesertEagle",
            }
            if not (ASSETS[skins.Bat] and ASSETS[skins.Bat].cat == "Bat") then skins.Bat = "DiamondSword" end
            if not (ASSETS[skins.Medusa] and ASSETS[skins.Medusa].cat == "Medusa") then skins.Medusa = "GoldenDesertEagle" end
            KCT.skinBat, KCT.skinMedusa = skins.Bat, skins.Medusa
        KCT.on = ctOn
        KCT.sounds = csOn

            local ctTracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local csTracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local csHooked  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
            local ctOn = KCT.on == true
            local csOn = KCT.sounds == true
            local alive = true
            KCT.soundMode = (KCT.soundMode == "Bat" or KCT.soundMode == "Medusa") and KCT.soundMode or "Both"
            local function soundAllowed(cat)
                local m = KCT.soundMode
                return m == "Both" or m == cat
            end

            local function catOf(tool)
                local n = tool.Name:lower()
                if n:find("bat", 1, true) or n:find("slap", 1, true) then return "Bat" end
                if n:find("medusa", 1, true) then return "Medusa" end
                return nil
            end

            local function isReplica(d)
                return d.Name == "LocalReplica" or d:FindFirstAncestor("LocalReplica") ~= nil
            end

            local function buildReplica(handle, key)
                local old = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
                if old then old:Destroy() end
                local data = ASSETS[key] or ASSETS.DiamondSword
                local m = Instance.new("Model")
                m.Name = "LocalReplica"
                local part = Instance.new("Part")
                part.Name = "MainPart"
                part.CanCollide = false
                part.CanQuery = false
                part.CanTouch = false
                part.Massless = true
                part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                part.Transparency = 0
                part.Anchored = false
                part.Parent = m
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = data.mesh
                mesh.TextureId = data.tex
                mesh.Scale = data.scale
                mesh.Parent = part
                local w = Instance.new("Motor6D")
                w.Part0 = handle
                w.Part1 = part
                w.C0 = data.c0
                w.Parent = part
                m.Parent = handle
            end

            local function hidePass(tool, entry)
                for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture")) and not isReplica(d) then
                        if entry.hidden[d] == nil then entry.hidden[d] = d.Transparency end
                        if d.Transparency ~= 1 then d.Transparency = 1 end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function ctEnsure(tool, cat)
                local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if not handle then return end
                local entry = ctTracked[tool]
                if not entry then
                    entry = { hidden = {} }
                    ctTracked[tool] = entry
                end
                local rep = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
                local mp = rep and rep:FindFirstChild("MainPart")
                local motor = mp and mp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Motor6D")
                if not rep or not mp or not motor or motor.Part0 ~= handle or entry.skin ~= skins[cat] then
                    buildReplica(handle, skins[cat])
                    entry.skin = skins[cat]
                end
                hidePass(tool, entry)
            end

            local function ctTeardown(tool)
                local entry = ctTracked[tool]
                if not entry then return end
                local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                local rep = handle and handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
                if rep then pcall(function() rep:Destroy() end) end
                for d, tr in pairs(entry.hidden) do
                    if d.Parent then pcall(function() d.Transparency = tr end) end
                end
                ctTracked[tool] = nil
            end

            local function csPlay(entry)
                local cs = entry.sound
                if not cs or not cs.Parent then return end
                local d = entry.data
                if entry.timer then
                    pcall(task.cancel, entry.timer)
                    entry.timer = nil
                end
                pcall(function()
                    cs:Stop()
                    cs.TimePosition = d.skip or 0
                    cs.Volume = 1
                    cs:Play()
                end)
                if d.doublePlay and d.delay then
                    entry.timer = task.delay(d.delay, function()
                        entry.timer = nil
                        if not entry.alive then return end
                        if cs and cs.Parent then
                            pcall(function()
                                cs:Stop()
                                cs.TimePosition = d.skip or 0
                                cs.Volume = 1
                                cs:Play()
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local function csHook(entry, snd)
                if not snd:IsA("Sound") or snd.Name == "CustomSound" or csHooked[snd] or entry.muted[snd] ~= nil then return end
                entry.muted[snd] = snd.Volume
                csHooked[snd] = true
                local function trigger()
                    if not entry.alive then return end
                    if snd.Playing or snd.TimePosition > 0 then
                        pcall(function()
                            snd.Volume = 0
                            snd:Stop()
                        end)
                        csPlay(entry)
                    end
                end
                local c1 = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(trigger)
                local c2 = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TimePosition"):Connect(trigger)
                table.insert(entry.conns, c1)
                table.insert(entry.conns, c2)
                local c3
                c3 = snd.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                    if parent then return end
                    pcall(function() c1:Disconnect() end)
                    pcall(function() c2:Disconnect() end)
                    pcall(function() c3:Disconnect() end)
                    csHooked[snd] = nil
                    entry.muted[snd] = nil
                end)
                table.insert(entry.conns, c3)
                if snd.Playing then trigger() end
            end

            local function csEnsure(tool, cat)
                local entry = csTracked[tool]
                if not entry then
                    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                    if not handle then return end
                    local data = SOUNDS[cat]
                    local old = handle:FindFirstChild("CustomSound")
                    if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
                    local cs = Instance.new("Sound")
                    cs.Name = "CustomSound"
                    cs.SoundId = data.id
                    cs.Volume = 1
                    cs.Looped = false
                    cs.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.Inverse
                    cs.MaxDistance = 1000
                    cs.MinDistance = 1000
                    cs.Parent = handle
                    entry = { sound = cs, data = data, conns = {}, muted = {}, alive = true }
                    csTracked[tool] = entry
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do csHook(entry, d) end
            end

            local function csTeardown(tool)
                local entry = csTracked[tool]
                if not entry then return end
                entry.alive = false
                if entry.timer then
                    pcall(task.cancel, entry.timer)
                    entry.timer = nil
                end
                for _, c in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                if entry.sound then pcall(function() entry.sound:Destroy() end) end
                for snd, vol in pairs(entry.muted) do
                    csHooked[snd] = nil
                    if snd and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
                end
                csTracked[tool] = nil
            end

            local function scan()
                local seen = {}
                for _, c in ipairs({ LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"), LP.Character }) do
                    if c then
                        for _, tool in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                            if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                                local cat = catOf(tool)
                                if cat then
                                    seen[tool] = true
                                    if ctOn then
                                        pcall(ctEnsure, tool, cat)
                                    elseif ctTracked[tool] then
                                        ctTeardown(tool)
                                    end
                                    if csOn and soundAllowed(cat) then
                                        pcall(csEnsure, tool, cat)
                                    elseif csTracked[tool] then
                                        csTeardown(tool)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                for tool in pairs(ctTracked) do
                    if not seen[tool] or not tool.Parent then ctTeardown(tool) end
                end
                for tool in pairs(csTracked) do
                    if not seen[tool] or not tool.Parent then csTeardown(tool) end
                end
            end

            local api = {}
            function api.setTools(on)
                ctOn = on == true
                KCT.on = ctOn
                pcall(scan)
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
            function api.setSounds(on)
                csOn = on == true
                KCT.sounds = csOn
                pcall(scan)
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
            function api.setSkin(cat, key)
                if ASSETS[key] and ASSETS[key].cat == cat then
                    skins[cat] = key
                    if cat == "Bat" then KCT.skinBat = key else KCT.skinMedusa = key end
                    pcall(scan)
                    pcall(saveAllSettings)
                end
            end
            function api.setSoundMode(mode)
                KCT.soundMode = (mode == "Bat" or mode == "Medusa") and mode or "Both"
                pcall(scan)
                pcall(saveAllSettings)
            end
            function api.getSoundMode()
                return KCT.soundMode or "Both"
            end
            function api.shutdown()
                alive = false
                ctOn, csOn = false, false
                pcall(scan)
            end
            KCT.api = api
            KCT.setTools = api.setTools
            KCT.setSounds = api.setSounds
            KCT.setSkin = api.setSkin
            KCT.setSoundMode = api.setSoundMode
            KCT.getSoundMode = api.getSoundMode
            KCT.getSkin = function(cat) return skins[cat] end

            task.spawn(function()
                while alive do
                    task.wait(0.4)
                    if ctOn or csOn or next(ctTracked) ~= nil or next(csTracked) ~= nil then
                        pcall(scan)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- 4) EXPORT / IMPORT de la config + reset
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        KINGMSC.export = function()
            return {
                confuser = KCF.on == true,
                confuserSpeed = KCF.speed,
                confuserSteal = KCF.steal == true,
                antiBat = KAB.on == true,
                infJump = KAB.jump == true,
                antiBatRange = KAB.range,
                customTools = KCT.on == true,
                customSounds = KCT.sounds == true,
                soundMode = KCT.soundMode or "Both",
                skinBat = KCT.skinBat,
                skinMedusa = KCT.skinMedusa,
            }
        end

        KINGMSC.import = function(data)
            if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
            KCF.speed = math.max(0, tonumber(data.confuserSpeed) or KCF.speed)
            KCF.steal = data.confuserSteal == true
            pcall(kmsSetAntiDie, antiDieEnabled == true)
            pcall(kmsSetConfuser, data.confuser == true)
            KAB.range = math.clamp(tonumber(data.antiBatRange) or KAB.range, 100, 50000)
            if KAB.rangeBox then KAB.rangeBox.Text = tostring(KAB.range) end
            pcall(kmsAbSetJump, data.infJump == true)
            pcall(kmsAbSet, data.antiBat == true)
            if KCT.api then
                if data.skinBat then pcall(KCT.api.setSkin, "Bat", data.skinBat) end
                if data.skinMedusa then pcall(KCT.api.setSkin, "Medusa", data.skinMedusa) end
                if data.soundMode then pcall(KCT.api.setSoundMode, data.soundMode) end
                pcall(KCT.api.setSounds, data.customSounds == true)
                pcall(KCT.api.setTools, data.customTools == true)
            end
            return true
        end

        KINGMSC.resetAll = function()
            pcall(kmsSetConfuser, false)
            KCF.steal = false
            pcall(kmsAbSet, false)
            pcall(kmsAbSetJump, false)
            if KCT.api then
                pcall(KCT.api.setTools, false)
                pcall(KCT.api.setSounds, false)
            end
        end

        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        -- 5) PANEL: Confuser + Anti Bat en Combat y Custom Tools en Visuals
        -- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        mkSect(combatPage, "Confuser")
        KCF.setVisual = mkToggle(combatPage, "Confuser", function(on)
            pcall(kmsSetConfuser, on)
        end)
        KCF.setStealVisual = mkToggle(combatPage, "Confuser Auto (Steal)", function(on)
            KCF.steal = on == true
            if not on then KCF.stealApplied = false end
            pcall(saveAllSettings)
        end)
        do
            local row = mkRow(combatPage, 42)
            mkLabel(row, "Confuser Speed")
            KCF.speedBox = mkBox(row, KCF.speed, 50, 56, function(v)
                if v and v >= 0 and v < 2000 then
                    KCF.speed = math.floor(v + 0.5)
                    if KCF.speedBox then KCF.speedBox.Text = tostring(KCF.speed) end
                    pcall(saveAllSettings)
                end
            end)
        end
        KAB.setVisual = mkToggle(combatPage, "Anti Bat", function(on)
            pcall(kmsAbSet, on)
        end)
        KAB.setJumpVisual = mkToggle(combatPage, "Inf Jump", function(on)
            pcall(kmsAbSetJump, on)
        end)
        do
            local row = mkRow(combatPage, 42)
            mkLabel(row, "Anti Bat Range")
            KAB.rangeBox = mkBox(row, KAB.range, 56, 62, function(v)
                if v and v >= 100 and v <= 50000 then
                    kmsAbSetRange(v)
                end
            end)
        end
        mkSect(visualPage, "Custom Tools")
        KCT.setVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "Custom Tools", function(on)
            if KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setTools, on) end
        end)
        KCT.setSoundsVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "Custom Sounds", function(on)
            if KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setSounds, on) end
        end)
        do
            local row = mkRow(visualPage, 42)
            mkLabel(row, "Sound Mode")
            KCT.soundModeLabel = mkSelector(row, "Both", { "Both", "Bat only", "Medusa only" }, function(direction, update)
                local opts = { "Both", "Bat", "Medusa" }
                local labels = { "Both", "Bat only", "Medusa only" }
                local cur = (KCT.api and KCT.api.getSoundMode and KCT.api.getSoundMode()) or "Both"
                local idx = 1
                for i, key in ipairs(opts) do if key == cur then idx = i end end
                idx = (idx - 1 + direction) % #opts + 1
                if KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setSoundMode, opts[idx]) end
                update(labels[idx])
            end)
        end
        do
            local row = mkRow(visualPage, 42)
            mkLabel(row, "Medusa Skin")
            KCT.medusaSkinLabel = mkSelector(row, "Gold Eagle", { "Gold Eagle", "Skull" }, function(direction, update)
                local opts = { "GoldenDesertEagle", "Skull" }
                local labels = { "Gold Eagle", "Skull" }
                local cur = KCT.getSkin and KCT.getSkin("Medusa") or "GoldenDesertEagle"
                local idx = 1
                for i, key in ipairs(opts) do if key == cur then idx = i end end
                idx = (idx - 1 + direction) % #opts + 1
                if KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setSkin, "Medusa", opts[idx]) end
                update(labels[idx])
            end)
        end

        -- Arranque: aplica lo guardado (la config se carga después de buildGui)
        if KCF.on then pcall(kmsSetConfuser, true) end
        if KAB.on then pcall(kmsAbSet, true) end
        if KAB.jump then pcall(kmsAbSetJump, true) end
        if KCT.on and KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setTools, true) end
        if KCT.sounds and KCT.api then pcall(KCT.api.setSounds, true) end

        pcall(function()
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.falls4kNavidadRunning and gui do
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if KCF.setVisual then pcall(KCF.setVisual, KCF.on) end
                    if KCF.setStealVisual then pcall(KCF.setStealVisual, KCF.steal) end
                    if KAB.setVisual then pcall(KAB.setVisual, KAB.on) end
                    if KAB.setJumpVisual then pcall(KAB.setJumpVisual, KAB.jump) end
                    if KCT.setVisual then pcall(KCT.setVisual, KCT.on) end
                    if KCT.setSoundsVisual then pcall(KCT.setSoundsVisual, KCT.sounds) end
                end
            end)
        end)


    local configPage = contentPages["Settings"]

    mkSect(configPage, "Automation")

    local mkToggleOrange = mkToggle

    setInstaGrab = mkToggleOrange(configPage, "Auto Steal", function(on)
        CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED = on == true
        if on then pcall(startAutoSteal) else stopAutoSteal() end
        updateProgressBarVisibility()
        pcall(saveAllSettings, true)
    end)
    do
        local row = mkRow(configPage, 46)
        mkLabel(row, "Auto Steal Mode")
        autoStealModeBtn = mkSelector(row, autoStealMode, AUTO_STEAL_VARIANT_NAMES, function(direction, update)
            local index = (autoStealVariant - 1 + direction) % #AUTO_STEAL_VARIANT_NAMES + 1
            local enabled = CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED
            if enabled then stopAutoSteal() end
            setAutoStealMode(AUTO_STEAL_VARIANT_NAMES[index])
            update(autoStealMode)
            if enabled then startAutoSteal() end
        end)
    end

    do
        local row = mkRow(configPage, 42)
        mkLabel(row, "Steal Radius")
        radInput = mkBox(row, CONFIG.STEAL_RANGE, 50, 56, function(v)
            if v and v >= 5 and v <= 300 then
                CONFIG.STEAL_RANGE = _floor(v+0.5)
                Steal.StealRadius = autoStealVariant == 6 and 10 or CONFIG.STEAL_RANGE
                radInput.Text = tostring(CONFIG.STEAL_RANGE)
                saveAllSettings()
            end
        end)
    end

    mkSect(configPage, "Tools")
    do
        local row = mkRow(configPage, 44)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
        local pingBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        pingBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.8, 0)
        pingBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.1, 0)
        pingBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)
        pingBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pingBtn.Text = "OPEN PING LAGGER"
        pingBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 214, 175)
        pingBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        pingBtn.TextSize = 13
        pingBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        pingBtn.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", pingBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local pingStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pingBtn)
        pingStroke.Color = getThemeColor()
        pingStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        pingStroke.Transparency = 0.4
        pingBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(pingBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = getThemeColor()}):Play()
            TS:Create(pingBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)}):Play()
        end)
        pingBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(pingBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)}):Play()
            TS:Create(pingBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 214, 175)}):Play()
        end)
        pingBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local okPing, errPing = pcall(openKINGPingLagger)
            if not okPing then warn("[KING Ping Lagger] " .. tostring(errPing)) end
        end)
    end

    mkSect(configPage, "Music")
    do
        local row = mkRow(configPage, 44)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
        local musicBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        musicBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.8, 0)
        musicBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.1, 0)
        musicBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)
        musicBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        musicBtn.Text = "REDRUM 0:00"
        musicBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 214, 175)
        musicBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        musicBtn.TextSize = 13
        musicBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        musicBtn.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", musicBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        local musicStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", musicBtn)
        musicStroke.Color = getThemeColor()
        musicStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        musicStroke.Transparency = 0.4

        local function refreshMusicButton()
            local playing = kingFullRedrumSound and kingFullRedrumSound.Parent and kingFullRedrumSound.Playing
            musicBtn.Text = playing and kingFullRedrumSound.Name == "KINGRedrumFull" and "STOP REDRUM" or "REDRUM 0:00"
        end

        table.insert(kingMusicRefreshers, refreshMusicButton)

        musicBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(musicBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = getThemeColor()}):Play()
            TS:Create(musicBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)}):Play()
        end)
        musicBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(musicBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)}):Play()
            TS:Create(musicBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 214, 175)}):Play()
        end)

        musicBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local same = kingFullRedrumSound and kingFullRedrumSound.Name == "KINGRedrumFull"
            stopKINGFullRedrum()
            for _, refresh in ipairs(kingMusicRefreshers) do refresh() end
            if same then return end

            local sound = makeKINGRedrumSound(0, "KINGRedrumFull")
            if sound then
                pcall(function()
                    sound.TimePosition = 0
                end)
                kingFullRedrumSound = sound
                refreshMusicButton()
                sound.Ended:Connect(function()
                    if kingFullRedrumSound == sound then
                        pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end)
                        kingFullRedrumSound = nil
                        refreshMusicButton()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local songs = {
            {name="Gané",file="KING_Gane.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Gane%CC%81_spotdown.org%202.mp3"},
            {name="CARNIVAL",file="KING_Carnival.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/CARNIVAL_spotdown.org.mp3"},
            {name="Type Shit",file="KING_TypeShit.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Type%20Shit_spotdown.org.mp3"},
            {name="El Black",file="KING_ElBlack.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/El%20Black_spotdown.org.mp3"},
            {name="X.O.X.O",file="KING_XOXO.mp3",url="https://files.catbox.moe/jghp0f.mp3",coverUrl="https://image-cdn-fa.spotifycdn.com/image/ab67616d00001e02b18c2edd559993b96a961ffc"},
            {name="Selfish",file="KING_Selfish.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Selfish_spotdown.org.mp3"},
            {name="Me Paseo",file="KING_MePaseo.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Me%20Paseo_spotdown.org.mp3"},
            {name="Gassed Up",file="KING_GassedUp.mp3",url="https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Gassed%20Up_spotdown.org.mp3"},
        }
        local function refreshAll()
            for _,refresh in ipairs(kingMusicRefreshers) do refresh() end
        end
        for _,entry in ipairs(songs) do
            local song=entry
            local row=mkRow(configPage,44)
            local button=KINGUI.new("TextButton",row,{Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-8),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,4),
                Text=song.name.." 0:00",TextSize=13,Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold,TextColor3=WHITE,
                BackgroundColor3=INP,BorderSizePixel=0,AutoButtonColor=false,ZIndex=8})
            if song.coverUrl then
                button.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(44,4)
                button.Size=UDim2.new(1,-50,1,-8)
                local cover=KINGUI.new("ImageLabel",row,{Name="SongCover",Position=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6),
                    Size=UDim2.fromOffset(32,32),BackgroundTransparency=1,Image="",ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit,ZIndex=8})
                KINGUI.round(cover,5)
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        local loader=getcustomasset or getsynasset
                        if type(loader)~="function" or type(writefile)~="function"then return end
                        local file="KING_XOXO_Cover.jpg"
                        if not (type(isfile)=="function" and isfile(file))then
                            local data=game:HttpGet(song.coverUrl)
                            if type(data)~="string" or #data<512 then return end
                            writefile(file,data)
                        end
                        local asset=loader(file)
                        if cover.Parent then cover.Image=asset end
                    end)
                end)
            end
            KINGUI.round(button,6)
            KINGUI.stroke(button,getThemeColor(),.4,1)
            local loading=false
            local function refresh()
                local current=kingFullRedrumSound
                local playing=current and current.Parent and current.Playing and current.Name==song.file
                button.Text=loading and ("CARGANDO "..song.name) or (playing and "STOP " or "")..song.name..(playing and "" or " 0:00")
            end
            table.insert(kingMusicRefreshers,refresh)
            button.Activated:Connect(function()
                if loading then return end
                local current=kingFullRedrumSound
                local same=current and current.Name==song.file
                stopKINGFullRedrum()
                refreshAll()
                if same then return end
                local token=kingMusicRequest
                loading=true;refresh()
                task.spawn(function()
                    local ok,err=pcall(function()
                        local loader=getcustomasset or getsynasset
                        assert(type(loader)=="function" and type(writefile)=="function","custom assets unavailable")
                        local exists=type(isfile)=="function" and isfile(song.file)
                        if not exists then
                            local data=game:HttpGet(song.url)
                            assert(type(data)=="string" and #data>256,"audio download failed")
                            writefile(song.file,data)
                        end
                        local asset=loader(song.file)
                        if token~=kingMusicRequest then return end
                        local sound=Instance.new("Sound")
                        sound.Name=song.file;sound.SoundId=asset;sound.Volume=.85;sound.Looped=false
                        sound.Parent=SoundService;sound.TimePosition=0
                        kingFullRedrumSound=sound
                        sound.Ended:Connect(function()
                            if kingFullRedrumSound==sound then kingFullRedrumSound=nil;refreshAll() end
                            sound:Destroy()
                        end)
                        sound:Play()
                    end)
                    loading=false;refreshAll()
                    if not ok then warn("[falls4k Navidad Music] "..tostring(err))end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end

            if delDebounce then return end

                delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 0, 0)
                task.delay(2, function()
                    if delBtn and delBtn.Parent and deleteState == 1 then
                        deleteState = 0
                        delBtn.Text = originalDeleteText
                        delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)
                        delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 30, 50)
                    end
                end)
            elseif deleteState == 1 then
                delDebounce = true
                local invoked, success = pcall(resetToFactoryDefaults)
                success = invoked and success
                delBtn.Text = success and "SETTINGS RESET" or "ERROR"
                delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)
                delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 30, 50)
                deleteState = 0
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    if delBtn and delBtn.Parent then
                        delBtn.Text = originalDeleteText
                        delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(27, 30, 33)
                        delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 30, 50)
                        delDebounce = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    shell.Name = "ModernAutoGrab"
    shell.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(330, 48)

    local savedGrabPos = (type(loadAutoGrabDirectPos) == "function" and loadAutoGrabDirectPos())
        or (_G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions and _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions["ModernAutoGrab"])
        or savedProgressBarPos
    if type(savedGrabPos) == "table" then
        local xs = tonumber(savedGrabPos.xScale or savedGrabPos.XScale) or 0
        local xo = tonumber(savedGrabPos.xOffset or savedGrabPos.XOffset or savedGrabPos.x or savedGrabPos.X)
        local ys = tonumber(savedGrabPos.yScale or savedGrabPos.YScale) or 0
        local yo = tonumber(savedGrabPos.yOffset or savedGrabPos.YOffset or savedGrabPos.y or savedGrabPos.Y)
        if xo ~= nil and yo ~= nil then
            initHudPos = UDim2.new(xs, xo, ys, yo)
        end
    end
    shell.Position = initHudPos
    shell.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)

    shell.ClipsDescendants = true
    shell.Active = true
    shell.ZIndex = 50
    shell.Parent = gui

    KINGUI.round(shell, 14)
    KINGUI.hudScale = KINGUI.new("UIScale", shell, {Scale = math.clamp((stealProgressScale or 100) / 100, 0.5, 2)})

    local outline = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    outline.Name = "RoyalOutline"
    outline.Thickness = 1.6

    outlineGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 75)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)),
    })

    fill.Name = "ProgressFill"
    fill.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3)
