    local grabBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    grabBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    grabBtn.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    grabBtn.Text = ""
    grabBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    grabBtn.ZIndex = 55
    grabBtn.Parent = shell

    local dragging, dragStart, startPos, moved = false, nil, nil, 0
    local dragMoveConn, dragEndConn = nil, nil

    local function stopGrabDragConns()
        if dragMoveConn then pcall(function() dragMoveConn:Disconnect() end); dragMoveConn = nil end
        if dragEndConn then pcall(function() dragEndConn:Disconnect() end); dragEndConn = nil end
    end

    local function saveGrabPos()
        local posTable = {
            xScale = shell.Position.X.Scale,
            xOffset = shell.Position.X.Offset,
            yScale = shell.Position.Y.Scale,
            yOffset = shell.Position.Y.Offset,
            XScale = shell.Position.X.Scale,
            XOffset = shell.Position.X.Offset,
            YScale = shell.Position.Y.Scale,
            YOffset = shell.Position.Y.Offset,
            x = shell.Position.X.Offset,
            y = shell.Position.Y.Offset,
            X = shell.Position.X.Offset,
            Y = shell.Position.Y.Offset,
        }
        savedProgressBarPos = posTable
        _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions = _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions or {}
        _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions["ModernAutoGrab"] = posTable
        pcall(saveAutoGrabDirectPos, posTable)
        pcall(kingSaveFloatPositions)
        if type(saveAllSettings) == "function" then pcall(saveAllSettings, true) end
    end

    local function finishGrabDrag()
        if not dragging then return end
        dragging = false
        stopGrabDragConns()
        if moved > 4 or (startPos and (shell.Position.X.Offset ~= startPos.X.Offset or shell.Position.Y.Offset ~= startPos.Y.Offset)) then
            saveGrabPos()
        end
    end

    grabBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if uiLocked then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end

        dragging = true
        moved = 0
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = shell.Position
        stopGrabDragConns()

        dragMoveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
            if not dragging then return end
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local delta = inp.Position - dragStart
                moved = delta.Magnitude
                if moved > 4 then
                    shell.Position = UDim2.new(
                        startPos.X.Scale,
                        startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                        startPos.Y.Scale,
                        startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                    )
                end
            end
        end)

        dragEndConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            finishGrabDrag()
        end)
    end)

    local stealDisplayed = 0
    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not shell or not shell.Parent then return end
        outlineGradient.Rotation = (outlineGradient.Rotation + dt * 45) % 360
        fillGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(os.clock() * 1.5) * 0.12, 0)

        local target = stealHud and stealHud.value or 0
        if isStealing and autoStealStartedAt and autoStealStartedAt > 0 then
            target = math.clamp((_tick() - autoStealStartedAt) / math.max((Steal and Steal.HalfHoldMin) or 0.25, 0.01), 0, 1)
        end
        stealDisplayed = stealDisplayed + (target - stealDisplayed) * math.clamp(dt * 10, 0, 1)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(stealDisplayed, 0, 1), -6 * math.clamp(stealDisplayed, 0, 1), 1, -6)
        progressPct.Text = _floor(math.clamp(stealDisplayed, 0, 1) * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
        local ready = stealDisplayed >= 0.87 or (stealHud and stealHud.state == "COMPLETE")
        local curState = (stealHud and stealHud.state) or (ready and "READY" or "UNREADY")
        progressStatus.Text = ready and (curState == "COMPLETE" and "COMPLETE" or "READY 👑") or (curState == "APPROACH" and "APPROACH" or curState)
        progressStatus.TextColor3 = ready and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
        accent.BackgroundColor3 = ready and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 65, 30)
    end)

    pbFrame = shell
    progressFill = fill
    updateStealProgress(stealHud.value, stealHud.state)
    KINGUI.fit()
    do
        local viewConnection
        local function followCamera()
            if viewConnection then viewConnection:Disconnect() end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then viewConnection = cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(KINGUI.fit) end
            KINGUI.fit()
        end
        local cameraConnection = Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(followCamera)
        followCamera()
        gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
            cameraConnection:Disconnect()
            if viewConnection then viewConnection:Disconnect() end
        end)
    end
    KINGUI.effectsLoop(gui)
    task.spawn(function() pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync({bg}) end) end)
end

do
    
    local function findGlobalFunc(...)
        local sources = {}
        pcall(function() if getgenv then table.insert(sources, getgenv()) end end)
        pcall(function() table.insert(sources, _G) end)
        pcall(function() if getfenv then table.insert(sources, getfenv()) end end)
        for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
            local name = select(i, ...)
            for _, env in ipairs(sources) do
                if type(env) == "table" then
                    local ok, val = pcall(function() return env[name] end)
                    if ok and type(val) == "function" then return val end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local setHiddenProp = findGlobalFunc(
        "sethiddenproperty", "set_hidden_property",
        "sethiddenprop", "set_hidden_prop"
    )
    local function setHidden(instance, prop, value)
        if not instance then return false end
        if setHiddenProp then
            local ok = pcall(setHiddenProp, instance, prop, value)
            if ok then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local TP = {
        enabled   = false,
        conn      = nil,
        char      = nil,
        h         = nil,
        hrp       = nil,
        cooldown  = false,
        
        batSpeed     = 40,   
        currentVersion = "V1", 
        silentAim    = false,
    }

    local function isBatTool(tool)
        if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
        local n = string.lower(tool.Name)
        return n:find("bat", 1, true) ~= nil or n:find("slap", 1, true) ~= nil
    end

    local function getBat()
        local c = LP.Character or TP.char
        if not c then return nil end
        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
            if isBatTool(ch) then return ch end
        end
        local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if bp then
            for _, ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if isBatTool(ch) then
                    if hum then
                        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(ch) end)
                    else
                        pcall(function() ch.Parent = c end)
                    end
                    return ch
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getHitDelay()
        local t = 0.12 - ((TP.batSpeed - 10) / 70) * 0.105
        return math.clamp(t, 0.015, 0.12)
    end

    local function tryHit()
        if TP.cooldown then return end
        TP.cooldown = true
        pcall(function()
            local bat = getBat()
            if not bat then return end
            pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
            for _, d in ipairs(bat:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("RemoteEvent") then pcall(function() d:FireServer() end) end
            end
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then pcall(function() ev:FireServer() end) end
        end)
        task.delay(getHitDelay(), function() TP.cooldown = false end)
    end

    local function getClosest()
        local myHrp = TP.hrp
        if not myHrp or not myHrp.Parent then
            local c = LP.Character
            myHrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            TP.hrp = myHrp
        end
        if not myHrp then return nil, math.huge end
        local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LP and p.Character then
                local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local tr  = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 and tr and tr.Parent then
                    local d = (myHrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = p end
                end
            end
        end
        return best, bestDist
    end

    local function setupChar(newChar)
        TP.char = newChar
        task.wait(0.1)
        TP.h   = newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        TP.hrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    end

    local function stopBatV2()
        TP.enabled = false
        if TP.conn then TP.conn:Disconnect(); TP.conn = nil end
        TP.char = nil; TP.h = nil; TP.hrp = nil
        TP.cooldown = false
        if _unsuppressBodyLock then pcall(_unsuppressBodyLock, true) end
    end

    local function startBatV2()
        if autoBatEnabled then disableAutoBat() end
        stopBatV2()
        TP.enabled = true
        TP.char = LP.Character
        if TP.char then
            TP.h   = TP.char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            TP.hrp = TP.char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        if _suppressBodyLock then pcall(_suppressBodyLock) end

        TP.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not TP.enabled then return end
            local c = LP.Character
            if not c then return end
            TP.char = c
            TP.hrp  = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            TP.h    = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not TP.hrp or not TP.h or TP.h.Health <= 0 then return end

            local target, dist = getClosest()
            if not target or not target.Character then return end
            local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not tr then return end

            setHidden(TP.hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr)

            local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
            if dist > 4 then
                TP.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, tr.Position)
            elseif dist > 1.5 then
                TP.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(TP.hrp.Position:Lerp(targetPos, 0.55), tr.Position)
            else
                TP.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos, tr.Position)
            end

            pcall(function() TP.hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)

            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not TP.silentAim and cam then
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
            end

            if TP.currentVersion == "V1" then
                tryHit()
            end
        end)
    end

    _G.__falls4kNavidadStartBatV2 = startBatV2
    _G.__falls4kNavidadStopBatV2  = stopBatV2
    _G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2    = function() return TP.enabled == true end
    _G.__falls4kNavidadBatV2      = TP

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        task.wait(0.5)
        setupChar(newChar)
        if TP.enabled then
            startBatV2()
        end
    end)
    if LP.Character then task.spawn(function() setupChar(LP.Character) end) end
end

do
    -- ============================================================
    -- KING FLOATING BUTTONS & TOP-CENTER FLOATS (Halloween Accent)
    -- ============================================================
    function getFloatBtnMetrics()
        local FLOAT_BTN_BASE = 60
        local FLOAT_BTN_GAP_BASE = 8
        local FLOAT_BTN_TEXT_BASE = 10
        local FLOAT_BTN_CORNER_BASE = 18
        local s = math.clamp(tonumber(floatBtnScale or (floatingButtonScale and floatingButtonScale * 100)) or 100, 50, 200) / 100
        local btn = math.max(32, math.floor(FLOAT_BTN_BASE * s + 0.5))
        local gap = math.max(4, math.floor(FLOAT_BTN_GAP_BASE * s + 0.5))
        local text = math.max(8, math.floor(FLOAT_BTN_TEXT_BASE * s + 0.5))
        local corner = math.max(8, math.floor(FLOAT_BTN_CORNER_BASE * s + 0.5))
        return btn, gap, text, corner, s
    end

    function setFloatBtnScale(pct)
        local n = tonumber(pct)
        if n == nil or n < 50 or n > 200 then return floatBtnScale or 100 end
        floatBtnScale = n
        floatingButtonScale = n / 100
        pcall(function()
            if type(createFloatingButtonsPanel) == "function" then createFloatingButtonsPanel() end
            if type(createTopCenterFloats) == "function" then createTopCenterFloats() end
        end)
        _G.setFloatBtnScale = setFloatBtnScale
        return floatBtnScale
    end
    _G.setFloatBtnScale = setFloatBtnScale

    function createFloatingButtonsPanel()
        pcall(function()
            local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            for _, n in ipairs({"falls4kNavidad_MobilePanel", "falls4kNavidadMobilePanel"}) do
                local old = cg:FindFirstChild(n)
                if old then old:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                for _, n in ipairs({"falls4kNavidad_MobilePanel", "falls4kNavidadMobilePanel"}) do
                    local old = pg:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if old then old:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end)

        local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        panel.Name = "falls4kNavidad_MobilePanel"
        panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
        panel.DisplayOrder = 120
        panel.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
        if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
            panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        end

        local BTN_W, GAP, TEXT_SZ, CORNER = getFloatBtnMetrics()
        local BTN_H = BTN_W
        local COLUMNS = 2
        local ROWS = 4
        local PANEL_W = BTN_W * COLUMNS + GAP * (COLUMNS - 1)
        local PANEL_H = BTN_H * ROWS + (GAP + 10) * (ROWS - 1)

        local container = Instance.new("Frame")
        container.Name = "FloatingPanel"
        container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.BorderSizePixel = 0
        container.Active = false
        container.Selectable = false
        container.Parent = panel

        local btnContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
        btnContainer.Name = "ButtonsContainer"
        btnContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        btnContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        btnContainer.Active = false
        btnContainer.Selectable = false
        btnContainer.Parent = container

        local INACTIVE_BG = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16)
        local ACTIVE_BG = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
        local INACTIVE_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
        local ACTIVE_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14)
        local STROKE_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        local ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 70)

        local buttonDefs = {
            { name = "DropBR", text = "DROP\nBR", order = 0 },
            { name = "AutoLeft", text = "AUTO\nLEFT", order = 1 },
            { name = "AutoRight", text = "AUTO\nRIGHT", order = 2 },
            { name = "AutoBat", text = "BAT\nAIMBOT", order = 3 },
            { name = "TpDown", text = "TP\nDOWN", order = 4 },
            { name = "Carry", text = "CARRY\nSPD", order = 5 },
            { name = "Lagger1", text = "LAGGER\n1", order = 6 },
            { name = "Lagger2", text = "LAGGER\n2", order = 7 },
        }

        local function getPos(order)
            local row = math.floor(order / 2)
            local col = order % 2
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            local vw = (cam and cam.ViewportSize.X) or 1920
            local baseX = vw - (PANEL_W + 10)
            local baseY = 12
            local x = baseX + col * (BTN_W + GAP)
            local y = baseY + row * (BTN_H + GAP + 10)
            return x, y
        end

        local buttonSetters = {}

        local function createButton(def, onClick)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
            btn.Name = def.name
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, BTN_W, 0, BTN_H)
            local dx, dy = getPos(def.order)
            local savedPos = _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions and _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions[def.name]
            if not savedPos and savedButtonPositions then
                savedPos = savedButtonPositions[def.name]
            end
            if type(savedPos) == "table" then
                local xs = tonumber(savedPos.xScale or savedPos.XScale) or 0
                local xo = tonumber(savedPos.xOffset or savedPos.XOffset or savedPos.x or savedPos.X)
                local ys = tonumber(savedPos.yScale or savedPos.YScale) or 0
                local yo = tonumber(savedPos.yOffset or savedPos.YOffset or savedPos.y or savedPos.Y)
                if xo ~= nil and yo ~= nil then
                    btn.Position = UDim2.new(xs, xo, ys, yo)
                else
                    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, dx, 0, dy)
                end
            else
                btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, dx, 0, dy)
            end
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = INACTIVE_BG
            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text = ""
            btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            btn.ZIndex = 30
            btn.Parent = btnContainer
            Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER)

            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
            stroke.Name = "NormalStroke"
            stroke.Color = STROKE_COLOR
            stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            stroke.Transparency = 0

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = def.text
            label.TextColor3 = INACTIVE_TEXT
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            label.TextSize = TEXT_SZ
            label.TextWrapped = true
            label.ZIndex = 31
            label.Parent = btn

            local active = false
            local function setActive(state)
                active = state and true or false
                if active then
                    TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundColor3 = ACTIVE_BG }):Play()
                    TS:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { TextColor3 = ACTIVE_TEXT }):Play()
                    TS:Create(stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { Color = ACCENT, Transparency = 0 }):Play()
                else
                    TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundColor3 = INACTIVE_BG }):Play()
                    TS:Create(label, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { TextColor3 = INACTIVE_TEXT }):Play()
                    TS:Create(stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { Color = STROKE_COLOR, Transparency = 0 }):Play()
                end
            end
            buttonSetters[def.name] = setActive

            local dragging, dragStart, startPos, moved = false, nil, nil, 0
            local dragMoveConn, dragEndConn = nil, nil
            label.Active = false
            label.Selectable = false
            btn.Active = true
            btn.Selectable = true

            local function stopDragConns()
                if dragMoveConn then pcall(function() dragMoveConn:Disconnect() end); dragMoveConn = nil end
                if dragEndConn then pcall(function() dragEndConn:Disconnect() end); dragEndConn = nil end
            end

            local function saveBtnPos()
                _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions = _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions or {}
                local posData = {
                    xScale = btn.Position.X.Scale,
                    xOffset = btn.Position.X.Offset,
                    yScale = btn.Position.Y.Scale,
                    yOffset = btn.Position.Y.Offset,
                    x = btn.Position.X.Offset,
                    y = btn.Position.Y.Offset,
                    X = btn.Position.X.Offset,
                    Y = btn.Position.Y.Offset,
                }
                _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions[def.name] = posData
                if savedButtonPositions then
                    savedButtonPositions[def.name] = { X = btn.Position.X.Offset, Y = btn.Position.Y.Offset }
                end
                pcall(kingSaveFloatPositions)
                if type(saveAllSettings) == "function" then pcall(saveAllSettings, true) end
            end

            local function finishBtnDrag()
                if not dragging then return end
                dragging = false
                stopDragConns()
                if moved > 6 then
                    saveBtnPos()
                end
            end

            btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                if uiLocked then return end

                dragging = true
                moved = 0
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = btn.Position
                stopDragConns()

                dragMoveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                    if not dragging then return end
                    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        local delta = inp.Position - dragStart
                        moved = delta.Magnitude
                        if moved > 6 then
                            btn.Position = UDim2.new(
                                startPos.X.Scale,
                                startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                                startPos.Y.Scale,
                                startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end)

                dragEndConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
                    if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                    finishBtnDrag()
                end)

                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        finishBtnDrag()
                    end
                end)
            end)

            btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if moved > 6 then return end
                if onClick then onClick(setActive, active) end
            end)

            return setActive
        end

        -- 1. DROP BR
        createButton(buttonDefs[1], function(setActive, active)
            if dropActive then return end
            executeDropWithToggle(function(state)
                setActive(state and true or false)
            end)
        end)

        -- 2. AUTO LEFT
        local setLeftBtn = createButton(buttonDefs[2], function(setActive, active)
            autoLeftEnabled = not autoLeftEnabled
            setActive(autoLeftEnabled)
            if autoLeftEnabled then startAutoLeft() else stopAutoLeft() end
            if autoLeftSetVisual then pcall(autoLeftSetVisual, autoLeftEnabled) end
        end)
        do
            local oldLeftVisual = autoLeftSetVisual
            autoLeftSetVisual = function(state)
                state = state and true or false
                if oldLeftVisual then pcall(oldLeftVisual, state) end
                setLeftBtn(state)
            end
        end
        mobSetAutoLeft = setLeftBtn
        setLeftBtn(autoLeftEnabled == true)

        -- 3. AUTO RIGHT
        local setRightBtn = createButton(buttonDefs[3], function(setActive, active)
            autoRightEnabled = not autoRightEnabled
            setActive(autoRightEnabled)
            if autoRightEnabled then startAutoRight() else stopAutoRight() end
            if autoRightSetVisual then pcall(autoRightSetVisual, autoRightEnabled) end
        end)
        do
            local oldRightVisual = autoRightSetVisual
            autoRightSetVisual = function(state)
                state = state and true or false
                if oldRightVisual then pcall(oldRightVisual, state) end
                setRightBtn(state)
            end
        end
        mobSetAutoRight = setRightBtn
        setRightBtn(autoRightEnabled == true)

        -- 4. BAT AIMBOT
        local setBatBtn = createButton(buttonDefs[4], function(setActive, active)
            if not autoBatEnabled then enableAutoBat() else disableAutoBat() end
            setActive(autoBatEnabled == true)
        end)
        mobSetAutoBat = setBatBtn
        setBatBtn(autoBatEnabled == true)

        -- 5. TP DOWN
        createButton(buttonDefs[5], function(setActive, active)
            doTpDown()
            setActive(true)
            task.delay(0.15, function() setActive(false) end)
        end)

        -- 6. CARRY SPD
        local setCarryBtn = createButton(buttonDefs[6], function(setActive, active)
            if not speedMode then
                speedMode = true
                laggerToggled = false
                laggerCarryToggled = false
                setActive(true)
                if buttonSetters.Lagger1 then buttonSetters.Lagger1(false) end
                if buttonSetters.Lagger2 then buttonSetters.Lagger2(false) end
            else
                speedMode = false
                setActive(false)
            end
            refreshSpeedModeLabel()
            if _G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow then pcall(_G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow) end
        end)
        mobSetCarry = setCarryBtn
        setCarryBtn(speedMode == true)

        -- 7. LAGGER 1
        local setLag1Btn = createButton(buttonDefs[7], function(setActive, active)
            if speedMode then speedMode = false; if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(false) end end
            if not laggerToggled then
                laggerToggled = true
                laggerCarryToggled = false
                setActive(true)
                if buttonSetters.Lagger2 then buttonSetters.Lagger2(false) end
            else
                laggerToggled = false
                setActive(false)
            end
            refreshSpeedModeLabel()
            if _G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow then pcall(_G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow) end
        end)
        mobSetLagger1 = setLag1Btn
        setLag1Btn(laggerToggled == true)

        -- 8. LAGGER 2
        local setLag2Btn = createButton(buttonDefs[8], function(setActive, active)
            if speedMode then speedMode = false; if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(false) end end
            if not laggerCarryToggled then
                laggerCarryToggled = true
                laggerToggled = false
                setActive(true)
                if buttonSetters.Lagger1 then buttonSetters.Lagger1(false) end
            else
                laggerCarryToggled = false
                setActive(false)
            end
            refreshSpeedModeLabel()
            if _G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow then pcall(_G.__falls4kNavidadApplySpeedNow) end
        end)
        mobSetLagger2 = setLag2Btn
        setLag2Btn(laggerCarryToggled == true)

        MobilePanel = panel
        return panel
    end

    function makeTopCenterFloat(name, text, xOffset, getState, onToggle)
        pcall(function()
            local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            local old = cg:FindFirstChild(name)
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                local old = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
                if old then old:Destroy() end
            end
        end)

        local panel = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        panel.Name = name
        panel.ResetOnSpawn = false
        panel.DisplayOrder = 121
        panel.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        panel.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        pcall(function() if syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(panel) end end)
        if not pcall(function() panel.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) then
            panel.Parent = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        end

        local BTN, _, TEXT_SZ, CORNER, scaleFactor = getFloatBtnMetrics()
        local scaledOffset = math.floor((tonumber(xOffset) or 0) * scaleFactor + 0.5)
        local btnFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        btnFrame.Name = "Frame"
        btnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, BTN, 0, BTN)
        btnFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)

        local savedPos = _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions and _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions[name]
        if not savedPos and savedButtonPositions then
            savedPos = savedButtonPositions[name]
        end
        if type(savedPos) == "table" then
            local xs = tonumber(savedPos.xScale or savedPos.XScale) or 0.5
            local xo = tonumber(savedPos.xOffset or savedPos.XOffset or savedPos.x or savedPos.X) or scaledOffset
            local ys = tonumber(savedPos.yScale or savedPos.YScale) or 0
            local yo = tonumber(savedPos.yOffset or savedPos.YOffset or savedPos.y or savedPos.Y) or 12
            btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(xs, xo, ys, yo)
        else
            btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, scaledOffset, 0, 12)
        end
        btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16)
        btnFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btnFrame.ZIndex = 30
        btnFrame.Parent = panel
        Instance.new("UICorner", btnFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, CORNER)

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btnFrame)
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.Thickness = 1.5

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = text
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        label.TextSize = TEXT_SZ
        label.TextWrapped = true
        label.ZIndex = 31
        label.Parent = btnFrame

        local function setActive(state)
            if state then
                btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245)
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 14)
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 70)
            else
                btnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 16)
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end
        setActive(getState and getState() or false)

        local clickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        clickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        clickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clickBtn.Text = ""
        clickBtn.ZIndex = 32
        clickBtn.Parent = btnFrame

        local dragging, dragStart, startPos, moved = false, nil, nil, 0
        local dragMoveConn, dragEndConn = nil, nil
        local function stopDragConns()
            if dragMoveConn then pcall(function() dragMoveConn:Disconnect() end); dragMoveConn = nil end
            if dragEndConn then pcall(function() dragEndConn:Disconnect() end); dragEndConn = nil end
        end
        local function saveTopPos()
            _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions = _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions or {}
            local posData = {
                xScale = btnFrame.Position.X.Scale,
                xOffset = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                yScale = btnFrame.Position.Y.Scale,
                yOffset = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset,
                x = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                y = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset,
                X = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset,
                Y = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset,
            }
            _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions[name] = posData
            if savedButtonPositions then
                savedButtonPositions[name] = { X = btnFrame.Position.X.Offset, Y = btnFrame.Position.Y.Offset }
            end
            pcall(kingSaveFloatPositions)
            if type(saveAllSettings) == "function" then pcall(saveAllSettings, true) end
        end

        local function finishTopDrag()
            if not dragging then return end
            dragging = false
            stopDragConns()
            if moved > 6 then saveTopPos() end
        end

        clickBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            if uiLocked then return end

            dragging = true
            moved = 0
            dragStart = inp.Position
            startPos = btnFrame.Position
            stopDragConns()

            dragMoveConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(m)
                if not dragging then return end
                if m.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or m.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    local delta = m.Position - dragStart
                    moved = delta.Magnitude
                    if moved > 6 then
                        btnFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                            startPos.X.Scale,
                            startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                            startPos.Y.Scale,
                            startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                        )
                    end
                end
            end)

            dragEndConn = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(e)
                if e.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and e.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
                finishTopDrag()
            end)

            inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    finishTopDrag()
                end
            end)
        end)

        local lastClick = 0
        clickBtn.Active = true
        label.Active = false
        clickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if moved > 6 then return end
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastClick < 0.12 then return end
            lastClick = now
            if onToggle then onToggle(setActive) end
        end)

        return panel, setActive
    end

    function createTopCenterFloats()
        local _, setV2 = makeTopCenterFloat(
            "falls4kNavidad_BatV2",
            "BAT\nV2",
            -36,
            function() return _G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2 and _G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2() or false end,
            function(setActive)
                if not _G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2() then
                    _G.__falls4kNavidadStartBatV2()
                    setActive(true)
                else
                    _G.__falls4kNavidadStopBatV2()
                    setActive(false)
                end
            end
        )
        mobSetBatV2 = setV2

        local _, setTP = makeTopCenterFloat(
            "falls4kNavidad_TPBat",
            "TP\nBAT",
            36,
            function() return TPBatState and TPBatState.enabled == true end,
            function(setActive)
                local nxt = not (TPBatState and TPBatState.enabled)
                if tpBatSetEnabled then tpBatSetEnabled(nxt) end
                setActive(nxt)
            end
        )

        local _, setFloat = makeTopCenterFloat(
            "falls4kNavidad_Float",
            "FLOAT",
            108,
            function() return floatEnabled == true end,
            function(setActive)
                if floatEnabled then
                    stopFloat()
                    setActive(false)
                else
                    startFloat()
                    setActive(true)
                end
            end
        )
        floatFloatSetVisual = setFloat
    end

    function createMobilePanel()
        pcall(kingLoadFloatPositions)
        local panel = createFloatingButtonsPanel()
        pcall(createTopCenterFloats)
        return panel
    end
end

batCounterV2Enabled = false
batCounterV2Debounce = false
batCounterV2Conn = nil
batCounterV2HitCooldown = false
BAT_COUNTER_V2_SWING_CD = 0.08
setBatCounterV2Visual = nil

local _batCounterV2OriginalTpState = false
local _batCounterV2SuppressCount = 0
local _lastBatCounterV2Time = 0
local _batCounterV2Cooldown = 0.01

local function isPlayerRagdolled(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    local state = hum:GetState()
    return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
end

local function hasAnimalEquipped(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    for _, child in ipairs(player.Character:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            if child:FindFirstChild("Handle") or child.Name:find("Animal") or child.Name:find("Pet") then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function getAttackingPlayerWithAnimal()
    local myChar = LP.Character
    if not myChar then return nil end
    local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil end
    local closest, minDist = nil, _huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            if not hasAnimalEquipped(plr) then continue end
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < 12 and dist < minDist then
                    minDist = dist; closest = plr
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

local function getBatV2Counter()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Tool") then
            local name = child.Name:lower()
            if name:find("bat") or name:find("slap") or name:find("sword") or name:find("knife") then return child end
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, child in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                local name = child.Name:lower()
                if name:find("bat") or name:find("slap") or name:find("sword") or name:find("knife") then
                    child.Parent = char
                    return child
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if bp then
        for _, child in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") then child.Parent = char; return child end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function tryHitBatCounterV2()
    if batCounterV2HitCooldown then return end
    batCounterV2HitCooldown = true
    pcall(function()
        local bat = getBatV2Counter()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then ev:FireServer() end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(BAT_COUNTER_V2_SWING_CD, function() batCounterV2HitCooldown = false end)
end

local function executeBatCounterV2()
    local now = _tick()
    if now - (_lastBatCounterV2Time or 0) < (_batCounterV2Cooldown or 0.01) then return end
    if batCounterV2Debounce then return end
    batCounterV2Debounce = true
    _lastBatCounterV2Time = now
    local attacker = getAttackingPlayerWithAnimal()
    if not attacker then batCounterV2Debounce = false; return end
    if isPlayerRagdolled(attacker) then batCounterV2Debounce = false; return end
    if not hasAnimalEquipped(attacker) then batCounterV2Debounce = false; return end
    local function doCounterHit()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local attChar = attacker.Character
        if not attChar then return end
        local attRoot = attChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not attRoot then return end
        if not hasAnimalEquipped(attacker) then batCounterV2Debounce = false; return end
        if sethiddenproperty then sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", attRoot) end
        local targetPos = attRoot.Position + _V3new(0, 0.9, 0)
        if (hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then hrp.CFrame = _CFnew(targetPos) end
        tryHitBatCounterV2()
        task.delay(0.05, function() tryHitBatCounterV2() end)
        task.delay(0.1, function() tryHitBatCounterV2() end)
    end
    doCounterHit()
    task.delay(0.2, function()
        batCounterV2Debounce = false
    end)
end

function stopBatCounterV2()
    if batCounterV2Conn then
        batCounterV2Conn:Disconnect()
        batCounterV2Conn = nil
    end
    batCounterV2Debounce = false
    batCounterV2HitCooldown = false
end

function startBatCounterV2()
    if batCounterV2Conn then return end
    batCounterV2Conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not batCounterV2Enabled then return end
        if batCounterV2Debounce then return end
        local character = LP.Character
        if not character then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local state = humanoid:GetState()
        local isBeingAttacked = state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp or
                               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Stunned
        if isBeingAttacked then
            local attacker = getAttackingPlayerWithAnimal()
            if attacker then
                if not isPlayerRagdolled(attacker) then
                    if hasAnimalEquipped(attacker) then
                        executeBatCounterV2()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function toggleBatCounterV2()
    batCounterV2Enabled = not batCounterV2Enabled
    if batCounterV2Enabled then startBatCounterV2() else stopBatCounterV2() end
    return batCounterV2Enabled
end

function updateUIFromLoaded()
    task.wait()
    if normalBox then normalBox.Text = tostring(NS) end
    if carryBox then carryBox.Text = tostring(CS) end
    if radInput then radInput.Text = tostring(CONFIG.STEAL_RANGE) end
    if laggerBox then laggerBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED) end
    if lagger2Box then lagger2Box.Text = tostring(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED) end
    if batSpeedBox then batSpeedBox.Text = tostring(BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) end
    if uiScaleBox then uiScaleBox.Text = tostring(uiScaleValue) end
    if dropModeBtnRef then dropModeBtnRef.Text = dropMode == 1 and "Fling" or "Jump Drop" end
    if bodyLockRangeBox then bodyLockRangeBox.Text = tostring(bodyLockRange) end
    if infJumpModeBtn then infJumpModeBtn.Text = string.upper(InfiniteJump.mode == "manual" and "manual" or "hold") end
    if autoStealModeBtn then autoStealModeBtn.Text = autoStealMode end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()

    local savedInfJumpEnabled = InfiniteJump.enabled == true
    InfiniteJump.stop()
    if savedInfJumpEnabled then
        InfiniteJump.start()
        if infJumpSetVisual then infJumpSetVisual(true) end
    elseif infJumpSetVisual then
        infJumpSetVisual(false)
    end

    for _, ref in ipairs(keyButtonRefs) do
        local entry = ref.entry
        local label = (entry.gp and entry.gp.Name) or (entry.kb and entry.kb.Name) or "None"
        ref.btn.Text = label
    end

    local hudPos = (type(loadAutoGrabDirectPos) == "function" and loadAutoGrabDirectPos())
        or savedProgressBarPos
        or (_G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions and _G.falls4kNavidadFloatBtnPositions["ModernAutoGrab"])
    if hudPos and pbFrame then
        local xs = tonumber(hudPos.xScale or hudPos.XScale) or 0
        local xo = tonumber(hudPos.xOffset or hudPos.XOffset or hudPos.x or hudPos.X)
        local ys = tonumber(hudPos.yScale or hudPos.YScale) or 0
        local yo = tonumber(hudPos.yOffset or hudPos.YOffset or hudPos.y or hudPos.Y)
        if xo ~= nil and yo ~= nil then
            pbFrame.Position = UDim2.new(xs, xo, ys, yo)
        end
    end

    applyBackgroundMode(backgroundMode)
    applyFloatingButtonScale()
    if KINGUI.fit then KINGUI.fit() end
    if KINGUI.motorLabel then KINGUI.motorLabel.Text = useVenixCarryEngine and "Carry Boost" or "Direct" end
    if KINGUI.carrySetter then KINGUI.carrySetter(KINGVenixCarry.softStealEnabled) end
    if KINGUI.carrySpeedBox then KINGUI.carrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(KINGVenixCarry.softStealSpeed) end
    if KINGUI.carryRadiusBox then KINGUI.carryRadiusBox.Text = tostring(KINGVenixCarry.softStealRadius) end
    if KINGUI.effectsSetter then KINGUI.effectsSetter(KINGUI.effects) end
    if KINGUI.lockButtonsSetter then KINGUI.lockButtonsSetter(mobileButtonsLocked) end

    if uiLocked and setLockUIVisual then setLockUIVisual(true) end

    if antiRagdollMode == "v1" or antiRagdollMode == "v2" then
        if _G.updateAntiRagdollUI then _G.updateAntiRagdollUI(antiRagdollMode) end
        setAntiRagdollMode(antiRagdollMode)
    else
        if _G.updateAntiRagdollUI then _G.updateAntiRagdollUI("off") end
    end

    if antiDieEnabled then
        if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(true) end
        AntiDieModule.start()
    else
        if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(false) end
    end

    if antiBatEnabled then
        if setAntiBatVisual then setAntiBatVisual(true) end
        startAntiBat()
    else
        if setAntiBatVisual then setAntiBatVisual(false) end
    end

    if antiFlingEnabled then
        if setAntiFlingVisual then setAntiFlingVisual(true) end
        startAntiFling()
    else
        if setAntiFlingVisual then setAntiFlingVisual(false) end
        stopAntiFling()
    end

    if CONFIG.AUTO_STEAL_ENABLED and setInstaGrab then setInstaGrab(true); pcall(startAutoSteal) end
    if autoBatEnabled then autoBatEnabled = false; enableAutoBat() end

    if medusaCounterEnabled then
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(true) end
        if LP.Character then setupMedusaCounter(LP.Character) end
    else
        if setMedusaVisual then setMedusaVisual(false) end
        stopMedusaCounter()
    end

    if batCounterEnabled and setBatCounterVisual then
        setBatCounterVisual(true)
        startBatCounter()
    end
    if batCounterV2Enabled and setBatCounterV2Visual then
        setBatCounterV2Visual(true)
        startBatCounterV2()
    end
    if unwalkEnabled and setUnwalkVisual then
        setUnwalkVisual(true)
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.5); startUnwalk() end)
    end
    if antiLagEnabled then
        if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(true) end
        enableAntiLag()
    else
        if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(false) end
        disableAntiLag()
    end
    if espEnabled then
        toggleESP(true)
        if setESPVIsual then setESPVIsual(true) end
    else
        toggleESP(false)
        if setESPVIsual then setESPVIsual(false) end
    end
    if espLineEnabled then
        if setESPLineVisual then setESPLineVisual(true) end
        startESPLine()
    else
        if setESPLineVisual then setESPLineVisual(false) end
        stopESPLine()
    end

    if vividGraphicsEnabled then
        enableVividGraphics()
        if setVividVisual then setVividVisual(true) end
    else
        disableVividGraphics()
        if setVividVisual then setVividVisual(false) end
    end

    if stretchEnabled then
        enableStretch()
        if _G.stretchToggleSetter then _G.stretchToggleSetter(true) end
    else
        if _G.stretchToggleSetter then _G.stretchToggleSetter(false) end
    end

    if mobSetAutoBat then mobSetAutoBat(autoBatEnabled) end
    if mobSetAutoLeft then mobSetAutoLeft(autoLeftEnabled) end
    if mobSetAutoRight then mobSetAutoRight(autoRightEnabled) end
    if mobSetCarry then mobSetCarry(speedMode) end
    if mobSetLagger1 then mobSetLagger1(laggerToggled) end
    if mobSetLagger2 then mobSetLagger2(laggerCarryToggled) end
    if mobSetBatV2 then mobSetBatV2(_G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2 and _G.__falls4kNavidadIsBatV2() or false) end

    if bodyLockEnabled and bodyLockSetVisual then
        if _blSuppressCount == 0 then
            bodyLockSetVisual(true)
            startBodyLock()
        else
            bodyLockSetVisual(false)
        end
    end

    updateProgressBarVisibility()
    startEnemySpeed()

    toggleLockUI(uiLocked)

    pcall(function() applyOutfitByIndex(currentOutfitIndex) end)
    if outfitSelectorLabel and OUTFITS[currentOutfitIndex] then
        outfitSelectorLabel.Text = OUTFITS[currentOutfitIndex].label
    end
end

local _bootErrors = {}

local function _bootSection(name, fn)
    print("[falls4k Navidad Boot] Iniciando:", name)
    local ok, err = pcall(fn)
    if not ok then
        print("[falls4k Navidad Boot] FALLÓ:", name, tostring(err))
        table.insert(_bootErrors, {name = name, err = tostring(err)})
        warn("[falls4k Navidad Boot] Error en '" .. name .. "': " .. tostring(err))
    else
        print("[falls4k Navidad Boot] OK:", name)
    end
    return ok
end

-- falls4k CHRISTMAS INTRO: pantalla de bienvenida con barra de carga, música y botón SKIP.
-- Coloca aquí el ID de un audio navideño que tengas permiso para usar.
-- Ejemplo de formato: "rbxassetid://1234567890"
local falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_ID = "rbxassetid://1838667764" -- Christmas Tree (instrumental)
local falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_VOLUME = 0.35

local function showfalls4kIntro()
    local introGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    introGui.Name = "falls4kChristmasIntro"
    introGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    introGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    introGui.DisplayOrder = 100000
    introGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    local parentOk = pcall(function() introGui.Parent = CoreGui end)
    if not parentOk then introGui.Parent = pgui end

    -- Música de la introducción: se detiene tanto al pulsar SKIP como al terminar.
    local introMusic = nil
    if type(falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_ID) == "string" and falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_ID ~= "" then
        introMusic = Instance.new("Sound")
        introMusic.Name = "falls4kChristmasIntroMusic"
        introMusic.SoundId = falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_ID
        introMusic.Volume = math.clamp(falls4k_INTRO_MUSIC_VOLUME, 0, 1)
        introMusic.Looped = false
        introMusic.Parent = SoundService
        introMusic.PlayOnRemove = false
        -- Inicia de inmediato; Roblox puede tardar un momento en cargar el audio.
        local playOk, playErr = pcall(function()
            introMusic:Play()
        end)
        if not playOk then
            warn("[falls4k Intro Music] No se pudo iniciar el audio:", tostring(playErr))
        end
        introMusic.Loaded:Connect(function()
            if introMusic and introMusic.Parent and not introMusic.IsPlaying then
                pcall(function() introMusic:Play() end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local black = Instance.new("Frame")
    black.Name = "Backdrop"
    black.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    black.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(3, 5, 5)
    black.BorderSizePixel = 0
    black.Parent = introGui

    -- Luces navideñas en la parte superior.
    local lightColors = {
        Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70), Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70),
        Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 130), Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255)

        light.Parent = black
        Instance.new("UICorner", light).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local glow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        glow.Color = light.BackgroundColor3
        glow.Thickness = 2
        glow.Transparency = 0.35
        glow.Parent = light
    end

    local skip = Instance.new("TextButton")
    skip.Name = "SkipButton"
    skip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    skip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 20)
    skip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(108, 38)
    skip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(17, 31, 25)
    skip.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    skip.BorderSizePixel = 0
    skip.Text = "SKIP  »"
    skip.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 239, 190)
    skip.TextSize = 14
    skip.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    skip.AutoButtonColor = true
    skip.ZIndex = 20
    skip.Parent = black
    Instance.new("UICorner", skip).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local skipStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    skipStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(205, 174, 93)
    skipStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    skipStroke.Thickness = 1
    skipStroke.Parent = skip

    local card = Instance.new("Frame")
    card.Name = "IntroCard"
    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.52)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0.76, 0, 0, 292)

    card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.Parent = black

    cardStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(190, 155, 75)
